`packages/ui/src/onboarding/StarterPagesList.tsx` :
```tsx
import { Badge } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/badge";
import { Checkbox } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/checkbox";
import { Input } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/input";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import type { SelectedPage } from "./presets";

const label = (c: { id: string; config: Record<string, unknown> }, titles: Map<string, string>) =>
  c.id === "tickets" && c.config.filter === "mine" ? fr.onboarding.mineTickets : (titles.get(c.id) ?? c.id);

type Props = { selection: SelectedPage[]; titles: Map<string, string>; onChange: (s: SelectedPage[]) => void };

export function StarterPagesList({ selection, titles, onChange }: Props) {
  const update = (key: string, patch: Partial<SelectedPage>) =>
    onChange(selection.map((p) => (p.key === key ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));
  if (selection.length === 0) return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{fr.onboarding.noPage}</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="divide-y rounded-lg border">
      {selection.map((p, i) => (
        <li key={p.key} className="grid grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,14rem)_auto_1fr] items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2">
          <Checkbox
            aria-label={fr.onboarding.include(p.title)}
            checked={p.checked}
            onCheckedChange={(v) => update(p.key, { checked: v === true })}
          />
          <Input
            aria-label={fr.onboarding.pageTitle(i + 1)}
            maxLength={40}
            value={p.title}
            disabled={!p.checked}
            onChange={(e) => update(p.key, { title: e.target.value })}
            className="h-8"
          />
          <Badge variant="outline">{fr.onboarding.kind[p.kind]}</Badge>
          <span className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {p.components.map((c) => label(c, titles)).join(" · ")}
          </span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```
`packages/ui/src/onboarding/RoleStep.tsx` :
```tsx
import type { Role } from "@kibo/schema";
import { Badge } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/label";
import { RadioGroup } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/radio-group";
import { Textarea } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/textarea";
import { ClipboardList, Code, Info, Palette, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react";
import { useAiAvailability } from "../ai/use-ai-availability";
import { ChoiceCard } from "../dialogs/ChoiceCard";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import { presetFor, ROLE_ORDER, type SelectedPage, toSelection } from "./presets";
import { StarterPagesList } from "./StarterPagesList";
import { useStarterSuggestion } from "./use-starter-suggestion";

const ICONS = { dev: Code, designer: Palette, pm: ClipboardList, other: Sparkles } as const;
const isRole = (v: string): v is Role => (ROLE_ORDER as string[]).includes(v);

type Props = {
  available: ReadonlySet<string>;
  titles: Map<string, string>;
  selection: SelectedPage[];
  onSelection: (s: SelectedPage[]) => void;
};

export function RoleStep({ available, titles, selection, onSelection }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>("dev");
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const { block } = useAiAvailability("assistant");
  const { state, suggest, cancel, reset } = useStarterSuggestion();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.status === "ready") onSelection(toSelection(state.plan));
    if (state.status === "unavailable") onSelection(toSelection(presetFor(role, available)));
  }, [state, role, available, onSelection]);

  const chooseRole = (value: string) => {
    if (!isRole(value)) return;
    setRole(value);
    reset();
    onSelection(toSelection(presetFor(value, available)));
  };

  const waiting = state.status === "waiting";
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4">
      <fieldset className="grid gap-2">
        <legend className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium">{fr.onboarding.role}</legend>
        <RadioGroup value={role} onValueChange={chooseRole} className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {ROLE_ORDER.map((r) => (
            <ChoiceCard key={r} value={r} icon={ICONS[r]} title={fr.onboarding.roles[r]} description={fr.onboarding.roleHelp[r]} />
          ))}
        </RadioGroup>
      </fieldset>
      {role === "other" && (
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label htmlFor={`${id}-usage`}>{fr.onboarding.usage}</Label>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fr.onboarding.optional}</span>
          </div>
          <Textarea
            id={`${id}-usage`}
            rows={2}
            maxLength={500}
            placeholder={fr.onboarding.usagePlaceholder}
            value={text}
            onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          />
          {text.trim().length > 0 &&
            (block ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{fr.ai.blocked[block]}</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Button type="button" variant="agent" disabled={waiting} onClick={() => suggest(role, text.trim())}>
                  <Sparkles className="size-4" /> {waiting ? fr.onboarding.suggesting : fr.onboarding.suggest}
                </Button>
                {waiting && state.queued && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fr.onboarding.queued}</span>}
                {waiting && (
                  <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={cancel}>
                    {fr.onboarding.cancelSuggestion}
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="grid gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-medium">{fr.onboarding.pages}</h3>
          {state.status === "ready" && (
            <Badge variant="secondary">
              <Sparkles className="size-3" /> {fr.onboarding.suggested}
            </Badge>
          )}
        </div>
        {state.status === "unavailable" && (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-muted px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            <Info aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> {fr.onboarding.unavailable}
          </p>
        )}
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fr.onboarding.pagesHelp}</p>
        <StarterPagesList selection={selection} titles={titles} onChange={onSelection} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`onSelection` passé par les parents est un setter d'état (`useState`), stable ; sinon l'envelopper dans `useCallback`.

`packages/ui/src/onboarding/StarterDialog.tsx` :
```tsx
import { Button } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/button";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/dialog";
import { toast } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/sonner";
import { useState } from "react";
import { client } from "../api";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import { applyStarterPlan } from "./apply-plan";
import { useStarterCatalog } from "./catalog-refs";
import { chosenPages, presetFor, toSelection } from "./presets";
import { RoleStep } from "./RoleStep";

type Props = { projectId: string; open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void };

export function StarterDialog({ projectId, open, onOpenChange }: Props) {
  const { refs, titles } = useStarterCatalog();
  const available = new Set(refs.keys());
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState(() => toSelection(presetFor("dev", available)));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const pages = chosenPages(selection);
  const submit = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const failures = await applyStarterPlan(client, projectId, pages, refs);
    setBusy(false);
    if (failures.length > 0) toast.error(fr.onboarding.partial);
    onOpenChange(false);
  };
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-2xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{fr.onboarding.suggestPages}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{fr.onboarding.roleSubtitle}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <RoleStep available={available} titles={titles} selection={selection} onSelection={setSelection} />
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
            {fr.common.cancel}
          </Button>
          <Button disabled={busy || pages.length === 0} onClick={submit}>
            {fr.onboarding.addPages(pages.length)}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

Dans `NewProjectDialog.tsx` :
- état `step: 1 | 2` (1 au départ), `selection` initialisée à `toSelection(presetFor("dev", available))` avec `useStarterCatalog()` ;
- étape 1 : en-tête « Nouveau projet » + `fr.onboarding.roleSubtitle`, `<RoleStep …/>`, pied « Passer » (ghost : décoche tout et passe à l'étape 2 avec « Projet vide ») et « Continuer » ; `fr.onboarding.step(1)` en petit texte à gauche du pied ;
- étape 2 : le formulaire actuel ; la carte « Projet dev » devient `ChoiceCard value="suggested" icon={LayoutDashboard} title={fr.onboarding.startSuggested} description={pages.map((p) => p.title).join(", ")} disabled={pages.length === 0}` ; `RadioGroup` contrôlé (`start`, `"suggested"` si au moins une page cochée, sinon `"empty"`) ; bouton « Retour » à gauche du pied ;
- `submit` : après `createProject`, si `start === "suggested"`, `const failures = await applyStarterPlan(client, p.id, pages, refs)` ; si `failures.length > 0`, `toast.error(fr.onboarding.partial)` ; puis fermer et `navigate(p.id)` (le projet existe quoi qu'il arrive).

`NewPageDialog.tsx` : prop `onSuggest?: () => void` ; sous le choix du type, si `onSuggest`, un `Button variant="link" size="sm"` « Suggérer des pages » qui ferme le dialogue puis appelle `onSuggest()`.
`ProjectHome.tsx` : prop `onSuggest: () => void` ; seconde ligne de la carte, même gabarit que la première : texte `fr.onboarding.suggestPagesHelp`, bouton outline `fr.onboarding.propose` (icône `Sparkles`).

`Shell.tsx` : état `suggestFor: string | null` ; `ProjectHome onSuggest={() => setSuggestFor(project.meta.id)}`, `NewPageDialog onSuggest={() => setSuggestFor(project.meta.id)}`, `{suggestFor && <StarterDialog projectId={suggestFor} open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && setSuggestFor(null)} />}`.

### Task 14: Cycle de vie d'un brouillon (démon)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-lifecycle.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-lifecycle.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createDraftGuard` (T3) ; `draftPaths`, `prepareDraft`, `copySource`, `verifyAndRestore`, `readDraftManifest`, `writePermissions`, `removeDraft` (T4) ; `generatorPrompt`, `fixPrompt`, `draftKiboFiles`, `GeneratorBrief` (T5) ; `applyDraftEvent`, `canRetry`, `DraftStore` (T6) ; ports (T1).
- Produces: `type LifecycleDeps = { store: DraftStore; runs: AgentRuns; devkit: Devkit; catalog: ComponentCatalog; ai: AiAvailability; events: AiEvents; clock: Clock; editor: Editor; home: string; sdkDir: string; args: () => string[]; env: () => Record<string, string>; newId: () => string }` ; `createDraftLifecycle(deps: LifecycleDeps): DraftLifecycle` avec `DraftLifecycle = { start(input: StartComponentDraftInput): Promise<ComponentDraft>; retry(draftId: string): ComponentDraft; revalidate(draftId: string): Promise<ComponentDraft>; abandon(draftId: string): null; openFolder(draftId: string): Promise<null>; list(): ComponentDraft[]; recover(): Promise<void>; idle(): Promise<void> }` ; `applyAndPublish(store, events, clock): (d: ComponentDraft, e: DraftEvent) => ComponentDraft` (applique, enregistre, publie `draft.changed`).

Codes d'erreur : identifiant pris ou brouillon actif pour ce composant ⇒ `CONFLICT` ; `claude` absent, non connecté ou profil `generateur` désactivé ⇒ `AI_UNAVAILABLE` ; composant à modifier inconnu, intégré ou de la marketplace ⇒ `NOT_FOUND` ; relance impossible ⇒ `INVALID_INPUT`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-lifecycle.test.ts` :
```ts
import { Database } from "bun:sqlite";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { type AiStatus, KiboError, type ValidationReport } from "@kibo/schema";
import { draftPaths, readDraftManifest } from "./draft-files";
import { createDraftLifecycle } from "./draft-lifecycle";
import { openDraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import type { AiAvailability, ComponentCatalog, Devkit, PublishedComponent } from "./ports";
import { createFakeClock, createFakeRuns, createRecordingEvents } from "./testing/fake-ports";

const roots: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => {
  for (const r of roots.splice(0)) rmSync(r, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

const report = (ok: boolean): ValidationReport => ({
  ok,
  manifest: { ok: true, errors: [] },
  imports: { ok: true, errors: [] },
  typecheck: { ok, errors: ok ? [] : ["ui.tsx(3,7): error TS2322"] },
  tests: { ok: true, passed: 1, failed: 0, output: "" },
  conformance: { ok: true, errors: [] },
  permissions: { declared: [], used: [], missing: [], unused: [], errors: [] },
  hash: null,
});

function setup(opts: { status?: Partial<AiStatus>; published?: PublishedComponent | null; reports?: boolean[] } = {}) {
  const home = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "kibo-life-"));
  roots.push(home);
  const sdkDir = join(home, "sdk");
  mkdirSync(sdkDir);
  const runs = createFakeRuns();
  const clock = createFakeClock();
  const events = createRecordingEvents();
  const store = openDraftStore(new Database(":memory:", { strict: true }));
  const reports = [...(opts.reports ?? [true])];
  const inferred: string[] = [];
  let inferError: KiboError | null = null;
  const devkit: Devkit = {
    scaffold: async ({ dir, id, title, kind }) => {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, "kibo.component.json"), JSON.stringify({ id, version: "0.1.0", kind, title, reads: [], writes: [] }));
      writeFileSync(join(dir, "ui.tsx"), "export const Component = () => null;\n");
      writeFileSync(join(dir, "component.test.tsx"), "runConformance({ manifest, Component });\n");
    },
    infer: async (dir) => {
      inferred.push(dir);
      if (inferError) throw inferError;
      return { reads: ["ticket"], writes: [], data: false, net: [] };
    },
    validate: async () => report(reports.shift() ?? true),
    hash: async () => "a".repeat(64),
  };
  const srcRoot = join(home, "components", "src");
  const catalog: ComponentCatalog = {
    entries: () => [],
    isTaken: (id) => id === "kanban" || existsSync(join(srcRoot, id)),
    sourceDir: (id) => join(srcRoot, id),
    latest: () => opts.published ?? null,
    usages: () => [],
    publish: async () => ({ id: "x", version: "0.1.0" }) as never,
    approve: async () => {},
  };
  const status: AiStatus = {
    available: true,
    reason: null,
    version: "2.1.283",
    loggedIn: true,
    profiles: { assistant: true, generateur: true },
    ...opts.status,
  };
  const ai: AiAvailability = { status: () => status, capabilities: () => null, refresh: async () => status };
  const opened: string[] = [];
  let n = 0;
  const life = createDraftLifecycle({
    store,
    runs,
    devkit,
    catalog,
    ai,
    events,
    clock,
    editor: { openFolder: async (dir) => void opened.push(dir) },
    home,
    sdkDir,
    args: () => ["--tools", "Read,Edit,Write,Glob,Grep,Bash"],
    env: () => ({ PATH: "/kibo/bin" }),
    newId: () => `0b5c1f3e-7a51-4d2a-9c1e-2f0d6f1b8a1${n++}`,
  });
  return { home, srcRoot, runs, events, store, life, inferred, opened, setInferError: (e: KiboError) => (inferError = e) };
}

const create = {
  mode: "create",
  id: "burndown",
  title: "Burndown",
  kind: "widget",
  withServer: false,
  description: "Burndown du sprint : tickets restants par jour.",
} as const;
const done = (sessionId = "s1") => ({ state: "done", sessionId, stdout: "", error: null }) as const;

describe("start", () => {
  test("refuses without AI, with a taken id, and with a second active draft", async () => {
    await expect(setup({ status: { available: false, reason: "missing" } }).life.start(create)).rejects.toThrow("AI_UNAVAILABLE");
    await expect(
      setup({ status: { profiles: { assistant: true, generateur: false } } }).life.start(create),
    ).rejects.toThrow("AI_UNAVAILABLE");
    const { life } = setup();
    await expect(life.start({ ...create, id: "kanban" })).rejects.toThrow("CONFLICT");
    await life.start(create);
    await expect(life.start(create)).rejects.toThrow("CONFLICT");
  });

  test("scaffolds the draft, writes the rules and enqueues a guarded generator run", async () => {
    const { home, runs, events, life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    const paths = draftPaths(home, d.id);
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: "generating", attempts: 1, runId: "run-1" });
    expect(readFileSync(join(paths.dir, "CLAUDE.md"), "utf8")).toContain("ui.tsx");
    const req = runs.runs[0]?.req;
    expect(req).toMatchObject({ profileId: "generateur", cwd: paths.dir, resumeSessionId: null, env: { PATH: "/kibo/bin" } });
    expect(req?.prompt).toContain("kibo component test .");
    expect(req?.guard({ toolName: "Write", toolInput: { file_path: join(paths.dir, "kibo.component.json") } }).decision).toBe("deny");
    expect(req?.guard({ toolName: "Write", toolInput: { file_path: join(paths.dir, "ui.tsx") } }).decision).toBe("allow");
    expect(events.events.at(-1)).toEqual({ type: "draft.changed", draftId: d.id, status: "generating" });
  });
});

describe("after the run", () => {
  test("restores reserved files, writes inferred permissions, validates, reaches review", async () => {
    const { home, runs, store, life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    const paths = draftPaths(home, d.id);
    writeFileSync(join(paths.dir, "kibo.component.json"), '{"id":"evil"}');
    runs.end("run-1", done());
    await life.idle();
    const after = store.get(d.id);
    expect(after).toMatchObject({ status: "review", sessionId: "s1", incidents: [{ kind: "restored", path: "kibo.component.json" }] });
    expect(readDraftManifest(paths.dir)).toMatchObject({ id: "burndown", reads: ["ticket"] });
    expect(store.report(d.id)?.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  test("a non-literal argument skips inference and lets the validation report it", async () => {
    const { runs, store, life, setInferError } = setup({ reports: [false] });
    setInferError(new KiboError("VALIDATION_FAILED", "non-literal argument"));
    const d = await life.start(create);
    runs.end("run-1", done());
    await life.idle();
    expect(store.get(d.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", failure: { kind: "validation" } });
  });

  test("a failed run is recorded; retry without session starts afresh", async () => {
    const { runs, store, life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    runs.end("run-1", { state: "failed", sessionId: null, stdout: "", error: "exit 1" });
    await life.idle();
    expect(store.get(d.id).failure).toEqual({ kind: "run_failed", detail: "exit 1" });
    life.retry(d.id);
    expect(runs.runs[1]?.req.resumeSessionId).toBeNull();
  });

  test("retry resumes the session with the report, three attempts at most", async () => {
    const { runs, store, life } = setup({ reports: [false, false, false] });
    const d = await life.start(create);
    for (const [i, runId] of ["run-1", "run-2", "run-3"].entries()) {
      runs.end(runId, done());
      await life.idle();
      expect(store.get(d.id).status).toBe("failed");
      if (i < 2) {
        life.retry(d.id);
        expect(runs.runs[i + 1]?.req).toMatchObject({ resumeSessionId: "s1" });
        expect(runs.runs[i + 1]?.req.prompt).toContain("error TS2322");
      }
    }
    expect(store.get(d.id).attempts).toBe(3);
    expect(() => life.retry(d.id)).toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
    expect(runs.runs).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  test("retry while generating is refused", async () => {
    const { life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    expect(() => life.retry(d.id)).toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
  });
});

describe("abandon, folder, revalidate, recover", () => {
  test("abandon cancels the run, removes the folders, ignores the late end", async () => {
    const { home, runs, store, life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    life.abandon(d.id);
    expect(runs.cancelled).toEqual(["run-1"]);
    expect(existsSync(draftPaths(home, d.id).dir)).toBe(false);
    await life.idle();
    expect(store.get(d.id).status).toBe("abandoned");
    expect(() => life.abandon(d.id)).toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
  });

  test("openFolder opens the draft folder in the editor", async () => {
    const { home, life, opened } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    await life.openFolder(d.id);
    expect(opened).toEqual([draftPaths(home, d.id).dir]);
  });

  test("modify: copies the source; a hand-made config change fails revalidation", async () => {
    const published: PublishedComponent = {
      version: "0.1.0",
      manifest: { id: "burndown", version: "0.1.0", kind: "widget", title: "Burndown", reads: ["ticket"], writes: [], data: false, net: [], configVersion: 0, changes: [], sdk: 1 },
      granted: { reads: ["ticket"], writes: [], data: false, net: [] },
      origin: "ai",
    };
    const { home, srcRoot, runs, store, life } = setup({ published, reports: [false] });
    mkdirSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown"), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown", "kibo.component.json"), JSON.stringify(published.manifest));
    writeFileSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown", "ui.tsx"), "old");
    writeFileSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown", "component.test.tsx"), "runConformance(x);\n");
    const d = await life.start({ mode: "modify", id: "burndown", description: "Ajoute un titre" });
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ mode: "modify", baseVersion: "0.1.0", title: "Burndown", withServer: false });
    runs.end("run-1", done());
    await life.idle();
    const paths = draftPaths(home, d.id);
    const m = readDraftManifest(paths.dir);
    writeFileSync(join(paths.dir, "kibo.component.json"), JSON.stringify({ ...m, configVersion: 1 }));
    await life.revalidate(d.id);
    expect(store.get(d.id).failure).toEqual({ kind: "config_changed", detail: null });
  });

  test("recover: a lost run fails as interrupted and reserved files are restored", async () => {
    const { home, runs, store, life } = setup();
    const d = await life.start(create);
    const paths = draftPaths(home, d.id);
    writeFileSync(join(paths.dir, "kibo.component.json"), "{}");
    runs.runs.splice(0);
    await life.recover();
    expect(store.get(d.id)).toMatchObject({
      status: "failed",
      failure: { kind: "interrupted" },
      incidents: [{ kind: "restored", path: "kibo.component.json" }],
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-lifecycle.ts` :
```ts
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { type ComponentDraft, KiboError, type StartComponentDraftInput } from "@kibo/schema";
import {
  copySource,
  type DraftPaths,
  draftPaths,
  prepareDraft,
  readDraftManifest,
  removeDraft,
  verifyAndRestore,
  writePermissions,
} from "./draft-files";
import { createDraftGuard } from "./draft-guard";
import { applyDraftEvent, canRetry, type DraftEvent, isActive } from "./draft-machine";
import type { DraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import type { AgentRuns, AiAvailability, AiEvents, Clock, ComponentCatalog, Devkit, Editor, RunEnd } from "./ports";
import { draftKiboFiles, fixPrompt, type GeneratorBrief, generatorPrompt } from "./prompts";

export type LifecycleDeps = {
  store: DraftStore;
  runs: AgentRuns;
  devkit: Devkit;
  catalog: ComponentCatalog;
  ai: AiAvailability;
  events: AiEvents;
  clock: Clock;
  editor: Editor;
  home: string;
  sdkDir: string;
  args: () => string[];
  env: () => Record<string, string>;
  newId: () => string;
};

export type DraftLifecycle = {
  start(input: StartComponentDraftInput): Promise<ComponentDraft>;
  retry(draftId: string): ComponentDraft;
  revalidate(draftId: string): Promise<ComponentDraft>;
  abandon(draftId: string): null;
  openFolder(draftId: string): Promise<null>;
  list(): ComponentDraft[];
  recover(): Promise<void>;
  idle(): Promise<void>;
};

const LIVE_RUN = new Set(["queued", "starting", "running", "waiting_input"]);

export const applyAndPublish =
  (store: DraftStore, events: AiEvents, clock: Clock) =>
  (d: ComponentDraft, e: DraftEvent): ComponentDraft => {
    const next = applyDraftEvent(d, e, clock.now());
    if (next === d) return d;
    store.save(next);
    events.publish({ type: "draft.changed", draftId: next.id, status: next.status });
    return next;
  };

const brief = (d: ComponentDraft): GeneratorBrief => ({
  mode: d.mode,
  componentId: d.componentId,
  title: d.title,
  kind: d.kind,
  withServer: d.withServer,
  description: d.description,
  baseVersion: d.baseVersion,
});

export function createDraftLifecycle(deps: LifecycleDeps): DraftLifecycle {
  const apply = applyAndPublish(deps.store, deps.events, deps.clock);
  const paths = (d: ComponentDraft): DraftPaths => draftPaths(deps.home, d.id);
  const pending = new Set<Promise<void>>();
  const track = (work: Promise<void>) => {
    const p = work.catch((e: unknown) => console.error("[kibo-daemon] draft work failed", e));
    pending.add(p);
    void p.then(() => pending.delete(p));
  };

  const requireGenerator = () => {
    const s = deps.ai.status();
    if (!s.available) throw new KiboError("AI_UNAVAILABLE", `claude unavailable: ${s.reason ?? "unknown"}`);
    if (!s.profiles.generateur) throw new KiboError("AI_UNAVAILABLE", "generator profile is disabled");
  };

  const configChanged = (p: DraftPaths) => {
    const base = readDraftManifest(p.baseDir);
    const current = readDraftManifest(p.dir);
    return (
      base.configVersion !== current.configVersion ||
      JSON.stringify(base.configSchema ?? null) !== JSON.stringify(current.configSchema ?? null)
    );
  };

  const inferOrNull = async (dir: string) => {
    try {
      return await deps.devkit.infer(dir);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof KiboError && e.code === "VALIDATION_FAILED") return null;
      throw e;
    }
  };

  const stillValidating = (id: string) => {
    const d = deps.store.get(id);
    return d.status === "validating" ? d : null;
  };

  const validate = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    const d = stillValidating(id);
    if (!d) return;
    const p = paths(d);
    try {
      if (d.mode === "modify" && configChanged(p)) {
        apply(d, { type: "config_changed" });
        return;
      }
      const permissions = await inferOrNull(p.dir);
      if (permissions) writePermissions(p.dir, permissions);
      const report = await deps.devkit.validate(p.dir);
      const current = stillValidating(id);
      if (!current) return;
      deps.store.saveReport(id, report);
      apply(current, { type: "validated", ok: report.ok });
    } catch (e) {
      const current = stillValidating(id);
      if (!current) return;
      apply(current, { type: "validation_crashed", detail: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
    }
  };

  const onRunEnd = async (id: string, runId: string, end: RunEnd): Promise<void> => {
    const d = apply(deps.store.get(id), {
      type: "run_ended",
      runId,
      state: end.state,
      sessionId: end.sessionId,
      error: end.error,
    });
    if (d.status !== "validating" || d.runId !== runId) return;
    apply(d, { type: "restored", incidents: verifyAndRestore(paths(d), d.withServer) });
    await validate(id);
  };

  const watch = (id: string, runId: string) => {
    deps.runs.onEnd(runId, (end) => track(onRunEnd(id, runId, end)));
  };

  const launch = (d: ComponentDraft, prompt: string, resumeSessionId: string | null): ComponentDraft => {
    applyDraftEvent(d, { type: "enqueued", runId: "check" }, deps.clock.now());
    const p = paths(d);
    const runId = deps.runs.enqueue({
      profileId: "generateur",
      label: `Composant ${d.title}`,
      cwd: p.dir,
      prompt,
      args: deps.args(),
      env: deps.env(),
      resumeSessionId,
      guard: createDraftGuard({ draftDir: p.dir, readRoots: [deps.sdkDir], allowServer: d.withServer }),
    });
    const next = apply(d, { type: "enqueued", runId });
    watch(next.id, runId);
    return next;
  };

  const newDraft = (input: StartComponentDraftInput): ComponentDraft => {
    const now = deps.clock.now();
    const common = {
      id: deps.newId(),
      componentId: input.id,
      description: input.description,
      runId: null,
      sessionId: null,
      status: "describing" as const,
      attempts: 0,
      failure: null,
      incidents: [],
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    };
    if (deps.store.active().some((d) => d.componentId === input.id))
      throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", `a draft of ${input.id} is already open`);
    if (input.mode === "create") {
      if (deps.catalog.isTaken(input.id)) throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", `component id ${input.id} is taken`);
      return { ...common, mode: "create", title: input.title, kind: input.kind, withServer: input.withServer, baseVersion: null };
    }
    const latest = deps.catalog.latest(input.id);
    const src = deps.catalog.sourceDir(input.id);
    if (!latest || (latest.origin !== "user" && latest.origin !== "ai") || !existsSync(src))
      throw new KiboError("NOT_FOUND", `no user component ${input.id}`);
    return {
      ...common,
      mode: "modify",
      title: latest.manifest.title,
      kind: latest.manifest.kind,
      withServer: existsSync(join(src, "server.ts")),
      baseVersion: latest.version,
    };
  };

  const idle = async () => {
    while (pending.size > 0) await Promise.all([...pending]);
  };

  return {
    async start(input) {
      requireGenerator();
      const draft = newDraft(input);
      const p = paths(draft);
      try {
        await prepareDraft({
          paths: p,
          kiboFiles: draftKiboFiles(brief(draft)),
          fill:
            draft.mode === "create"
              ? (dir) =>
                  deps.devkit.scaffold({ dir, id: draft.componentId, title: draft.title, kind: draft.kind, withServer: draft.withServer })
              : async (dir) => copySource(deps.catalog.sourceDir(draft.componentId), dir),
        });
      } catch (e) {
        removeDraft(p);
        throw e;
      }
      deps.store.insert(draft);
      return launch(draft, generatorPrompt(brief(draft)), null);
    },

    retry(draftId) {
      requireGenerator();
      const d = deps.store.get(draftId);
      if (!canRetry(d)) throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "this draft cannot be retried");
      const report = deps.store.report(draftId);
      const prompt = report && !report.ok ? fixPrompt(report) : generatorPrompt(brief(d));
      return launch(d, prompt, d.sessionId);
    },

    async revalidate(draftId) {
      apply(deps.store.get(draftId), { type: "validation_started" });
      await validate(draftId);
      return deps.store.get(draftId);
    },

    abandon(draftId) {
      const d = apply(deps.store.get(draftId), { type: "abandoned" });
      if (d.runId && LIVE_RUN.has(deps.runs.state(d.runId) ?? "")) deps.runs.cancel(d.runId);
      removeDraft(paths(d));
      return null;
    },

    async openFolder(draftId) {
      const d = deps.store.get(draftId);
      if (!isActive(d)) throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `draft is ${d.status}`);
      await deps.editor.openFolder(paths(d).dir);
      return null;
    },

    list: () => deps.store.list(),

    async recover() {
      for (const d of deps.store.active()) {
        if (!existsSync(paths(d).baseDir)) {
          apply(d, { type: "abandoned" });
          continue;
        }
        if (d.status === "generating" && d.runId && LIVE_RUN.has(deps.runs.state(d.runId) ?? "")) {
          watch(d.id, d.runId);
          continue;
        }
        if (d.status === "describing" || d.status === "generating") {
          const failed = apply(d, { type: "interrupted" });
          apply(failed, { type: "restored", incidents: verifyAndRestore(paths(failed), failed.withServer) });
        }
        if (d.status === "validating") track(validate(d.id));
      }
      await idle();
    },

    idle,
  };
}
```
Un brouillon dont la base manque (arrêt pendant `prepareDraft`) passe `abandoned` au redémarrage ; ajouter le test « recover : brouillon sans dossier ⇒ abandonné » (supprimer `drafts/<id>.base` avant `recover`).

### Task 15: Relecture, version proposée et publication d'un brouillon

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-version.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-version.test.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-publish.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-publish.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `draftPaths`, `agentFiles`, `readDraftManifest`, `writeDraftManifest`, `installDraft`, `removeDraft` (T4) ; `applyDraftEvent`, `DraftStore` (T6) ; ports `Devkit`, `ComponentCatalog`, `ProjectAccess`, `Differ`, `AiEvents`, `Clock` (T1).
- Produces: `compareSemver(a: string, b: string): number` ; `bumpVersion(v: string, part: "minor" | "patch"): string` ; `permissionKeys(p: GrantedPermissions): string[]` (notation du devkit : `read:x`, `write:x`, `data`, `net:hôte/chemin`) ; `grantedFromKeys(keys: string[]): GrantedPermissions` (lève `VALIDATION_FAILED` sur une clé inconnue) ; `proposeVersion(base: { version: string; permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number } | null, next: { permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number }): string` ; `type PublishDeps = { store: DraftStore; devkit: Devkit; catalog: ComponentCatalog; projects: ProjectAccess; differ: Differ; events: AiEvents; clock: Clock; home: string }` ; `createDraftPublisher(deps: PublishDeps): { details(draftId: string): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails>; review(input: ReviewComponentDraftInput): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails>; finalize(input: FinalizeComponentDraftInput): Promise<PublishResult> }`.

T15 n'importe pas `applyAndPublish` de T14 (vagues parallèles) : il applique ses deux événements (`reviewed`, `finalized`) avec `applyDraftEvent` puis `store.save` et `events.publish`, en trois lignes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Tests de version**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-version.test.ts` :
```ts
import { expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { bumpVersion, compareSemver, grantedFromKeys, permissionKeys, proposeVersion } from "./draft-version";

const none = { reads: [], writes: [], data: false, net: [] };

test("compareSemver and bumpVersion", () => {
  expect(compareSemver("0.10.0", "0.9.9")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(compareSemver("1.0.0", "1.0.0")).toBe(0);
  expect(bumpVersion("0.1.3", "minor")).toBe("0.2.0");
  expect(bumpVersion("0.1.3", "patch")).toBe("0.1.4");
});

test("permissionKeys uses the devkit notation, grantedFromKeys reverses it", () => {
  const p = { reads: ["ticket" as const], writes: ["ticket" as const], data: true, net: ["api.github.com/graphql"] };
  expect(permissionKeys(p)).toEqual(["read:ticket", "write:ticket", "data", "net:api.github.com/graphql"]);
  expect(grantedFromKeys(permissionKeys(p))).toEqual(p);
  expect(() => grantedFromKeys(["read:bogus"])).toThrow("VALIDATION_FAILED");
});

test("proposeVersion: 0.1.0 on create, patch if nothing grows, minor otherwise", () => {
  expect(proposeVersion(null, { permissions: none, configVersion: 0 })).toBe("0.1.0");
  const base = { version: "0.1.0", permissions: { ...none, reads: ["ticket" as const] }, configVersion: 0 };
  expect(proposeVersion(base, { permissions: { ...none, reads: ["ticket"] }, configVersion: 0 })).toBe("0.1.1");
  expect(proposeVersion(base, { permissions: { ...none, reads: ["ticket"], net: ["api.github.com/x"] }, configVersion: 0 })).toBe(
    "0.2.0",
  );
  expect(proposeVersion(base, { permissions: none, configVersion: 0 })).toBe("0.2.0");
  expect(proposeVersion(base, { permissions: base.permissions, configVersion: 1 })).toBe("0.2.0");
});
```

```ts
import { Database } from "bun:sqlite";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { ComponentDraft, DraftStatus, FileDiff } from "@kibo/schema";
import { draftPaths, prepareDraft, readDraftManifest } from "./draft-files";
import { createDraftPublisher } from "./draft-publish";
import { openDraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import type { ComponentCatalog, PublishedComponent } from "./ports";
import { createFakeClock, createRecordingEvents } from "./testing/fake-ports";

const roots: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => {
  for (const r of roots.splice(0)) rmSync(r, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
const ID = "0b5c1f3e-7a51-4d2a-9c1e-2f0d6f1b8a11";
const hashOf = (dir: string) =>
  createHash("sha256")
    .update(["kibo.component.json", "ui.tsx"].map((f) => (existsSync(join(dir, f)) ? readFileSync(join(dir, f), "utf8") : "")).join("\0"))
    .digest("hex");
const manifest = { id: "burndown", version: "0.1.0", kind: "widget", title: "Burndown", reads: ["ticket"], writes: [], data: false, net: [], configVersion: 0, changes: [], sdk: 1 };

async function setup(opts: { mode?: "create" | "modify"; status?: DraftStatus; published?: PublishedComponent | null; publishFails?: boolean } = {}) {
  const home = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "kibo-pub-"));
  roots.push(home);
  const mode = opts.mode ?? "create";
  const srcRoot = join(home, "components", "src");
  if (mode === "modify") {
    mkdirSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown"), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown", "kibo.component.json"), JSON.stringify(manifest));
    writeFileSync(join(srcRoot, "burndown", "ui.tsx"), "old");
  }
  const paths = draftPaths(home, ID);
  await prepareDraft({
    paths,
    kiboFiles: { "CLAUDE.md": "# Règles\n" },
    fill: async (dir) => {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, "kibo.component.json"), JSON.stringify(manifest));
      writeFileSync(join(dir, "ui.tsx"), "old");
    },
  });
  writeFileSync(join(paths.dir, "ui.tsx"), "new");
  const store = openDraftStore(new Database(":memory:", { strict: true }));
  const draft: ComponentDraft = {
    id: ID,
    componentId: "burndown",
    mode,
    title: "Burndown",
    kind: "widget",
    withServer: false,
    baseVersion: mode === "modify" ? "0.1.0" : null,
    description: "Ajoute un titre\net une légende",
    runId: "run-1",
    sessionId: "s1",
    status: opts.status ?? "review",
    attempts: 1,
    failure: null,
    incidents: [],
    createdAt: 1,
    updatedAt: 1,
  };
  store.insert(draft);
  const published: string[] = [];
  const approved: unknown[] = [];
  const instances: string[] = [];
  const catalog: ComponentCatalog = {
    entries: () => [],
    isTaken: () => false,
    sourceDir: (id) => join(srcRoot, id),
    latest: () => opts.published ?? null,
    usages: () => [],
    publish: async (input) => {
      if (opts.publishFails) throw new Error("build failed");
      published.push(`${input.id}:${input.strategy}:${input.origin}`);
      return { id: input.id, version: readDraftManifest(join(srcRoot, input.id)).version } as never;
    },
    approve: async (input) => void approved.push(input),
  };
  const diffs: string[] = [];
  const publisher = createDraftPublisher({
    store,
    devkit: { scaffold: async () => {}, infer: async () => manifest as never, validate: async () => ({}) as never, hash: async (dir) => hashOf(dir) },
    catalog,
    projects: {
      pageExists: (projectId, pageId) => projectId === "p1" && pageId === "pg1",
      addInstance: async (_p, _pg, ref) => {
        instances.push(ref);
        return {} as never;
      },
    },
    differ: async ({ path }) => {
      diffs.push(path);
      return { path } as FileDiff;
    },
    events: createRecordingEvents(),
    clock: createFakeClock(),
    home,
  });
  return { home, srcRoot, paths, store, publisher, published, approved, instances, diffs };
}

const published: PublishedComponent = {
  version: "0.1.0",
  manifest: manifest as never,
  granted: { reads: ["ticket"], writes: [], data: false, net: [] },
  origin: "ai",
};

describe("details", () => {
  test("create: diff of changed agent files, version 0.1.0, every permission is new, no hash yet", async () => {
    const { publisher, diffs } = await setup();
    const d = await publisher.details(ID);
    expect(diffs).toEqual(["ui.tsx"]);
    expect(d.publish).toMatchObject({ from: null, proposedVersion: "0.1.0", newPermissions: ["read:ticket"], hash: null, changes: [] });
  });
  test("modify: patch proposed and the first line of the request as change", async () => {
    const { publisher } = await setup({ mode: "modify", published });
    expect((await publisher.details(ID)).publish).toMatchObject({
      from: "0.1.0",
      proposedVersion: "0.1.1",
      changes: ["Ajoute un titre"],
      newPermissions: [],
    });
  });
  test("no diff nor publish info before review", async () => {
    const { publisher } = await setup({ status: "failed" });
    expect(await publisher.details(ID)).toMatchObject({ diff: [], publish: null });
  });
});

describe("review", () => {
  test("writes version and changes, moves to permissions, exposes the hash", async () => {
    const { publisher, paths } = await setup({ mode: "modify", published });
    const d = await publisher.review({ draftId: ID, version: "0.1.1", changes: ["Ajoute un titre"] });
    expect(d.status).toBe("permissions");
    expect(readDraftManifest(paths.dir)).toMatchObject({ version: "0.1.1", changes: ["Ajoute un titre"] });
    expect(d.publish?.hash).toBe(hashOf(paths.dir));
  });
  test("refuses a version not above the published one, or a draft not in review", async () => {
    const { publisher } = await setup({ mode: "modify", published });
    await expect(publisher.review({ draftId: ID, version: "0.1.0", changes: [] })).rejects.toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
    const other = await setup({ status: "failed" });
    await expect(other.publisher.review({ draftId: ID, version: "0.1.0", changes: [] })).rejects.toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
  });
});

describe("finalize", () => {
  const input = (hash: string) => ({
    draftId: ID,
    version: "0.1.0",
    hash,
    trust: "sandboxed" as const,
    strategy: "update-all" as const,
    target: { projectId: "p1", pageId: "pg1" },
  });

  test("installs the source without Kibo files, publishes as ai, approves, adds the instance", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ status: "permissions" });
    await s.publisher.finalize(input(hashOf(s.paths.dir)));
    expect(readFileSync(join(s.srcRoot, "burndown", "ui.tsx"), "utf8")).toBe("new");
    expect(existsSync(join(s.srcRoot, "burndown", "CLAUDE.md"))).toBe(false);
    expect(s.published).toEqual(["burndown:update-all:ai"]);
    expect(s.approved).toEqual([{ id: "burndown", version: "0.1.0", hash: hashOf(join(s.srcRoot, "burndown")), trust: "sandboxed" }]);
    expect(s.instances).toEqual(["burndown@0.1.0"]);
    expect(s.store.get(ID).status).toBe("done");
    expect(existsSync(s.paths.dir)).toBe(false);
  });

  test("a changed draft is HASH_MISMATCH and nothing is published", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ status: "permissions" });
    await expect(s.publisher.finalize(input("b".repeat(64)))).rejects.toThrow("HASH_MISMATCH");
    expect(s.published).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("a double click publishes once", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ status: "permissions" });
    const hash = hashOf(s.paths.dir);
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([s.publisher.finalize(input(hash)), s.publisher.finalize(input(hash))]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual(["fulfilled", "rejected"]);
    expect(s.published).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test("a missing page is refused before publishing", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ status: "permissions" });
    await expect(
      s.publisher.finalize({ ...input(hashOf(s.paths.dir)), target: { projectId: "p1", pageId: "gone" } }),
    ).rejects.toThrow("NOT_FOUND");
    expect(s.published).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("modify: a failed publication restores the previous source", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ mode: "modify", status: "permissions", published, publishFails: true });
    await expect(s.publisher.finalize({ ...input(hashOf(s.paths.dir)), target: null })).rejects.toThrow("build failed");
    expect(readFileSync(join(s.srcRoot, "burndown", "ui.tsx"), "utf8")).toBe("old");
    expect(s.store.get(ID).status).toBe("permissions");
  });

  test("modify: a source edited since the draft started is CONFLICT", async () => {
    const s = await setup({ mode: "modify", status: "permissions", published });
    writeFileSync(join(s.srcRoot, "burndown", "ui.tsx"), "edited by hand");
    await expect(s.publisher.finalize({ ...input(hashOf(s.paths.dir)), target: null })).rejects.toThrow("CONFLICT");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implémenter**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-version.ts` :
```ts
import { GrantedPermissions, KiboError } from "@kibo/schema";

const parts = (v: string) => v.split(".").map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10));

export function compareSemver(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [pa, pb] = [parts(a), parts(b)];
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const diff = (pa[i] ?? 0) - (pb[i] ?? 0);
    if (diff !== 0) return diff;
  }
  return 0;
}

export function bumpVersion(v: string, part: "minor" | "patch"): string {
  const [major = 0, minor = 0, patch = 0] = parts(v);
  return part === "minor" ? `${major}.${minor + 1}.0` : `${major}.${minor}.${patch + 1}`;
}

export function permissionKeys(p: GrantedPermissions): string[] {
  return [
    ...p.reads.map((r) => `read:${r}`),
    ...p.writes.map((w) => `write:${w}`),
    ...(p.data ? ["data"] : []),
    ...p.net.map((n) => `net:${n}`),
  ];
}

export function grantedFromKeys(keys: string[]): GrantedPermissions {
  const reads = new Set<string>();
  const writes = new Set<string>();
  const net = new Set<string>();
  let data = false;
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (key === "data") data = true;
    else if (key.startsWith("read:")) reads.add(key.slice(5));
    else if (key.startsWith("write:")) writes.add(key.slice(6));
    else if (key.startsWith("net:")) net.add(key.slice(4));
    else throw new KiboError("VALIDATION_FAILED", `unknown permission ${key}`);
  }
  const parsed = GrantedPermissions.safeParse({ reads: [...reads], writes: [...writes], data, net: [...net] });
  if (!parsed.success) throw new KiboError("VALIDATION_FAILED", parsed.error.message);
  return parsed.data;
}

export function proposeVersion(
  base: { version: string; permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number } | null,
  next: { permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number },
): string {
  if (!base) return "0.1.0";
  const before = permissionKeys(base.permissions).sort().join("|");
  const after = permissionKeys(next.permissions).sort().join("|");
  return before === after && base.configVersion === next.configVersion
    ? bumpVersion(base.version, "patch")
    : bumpVersion(base.version, "minor");
}
```

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-publish.ts` :
```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import {
  type ComponentDraft,
  type ComponentDraftDetails,
  type ComponentManifest,
  type DraftPublishInfo,
  type FinalizeComponentDraftInput,
  type GrantedPermissions,
  KiboError,
  type PublishResult,
  type ReviewComponentDraftInput,
} from "@kibo/schema";
import {
  agentFiles,
  type DraftPaths,
  draftPaths,
  installDraft,
  readDraftManifest,
  removeDraft,
  writeDraftManifest,
} from "./draft-files";
import { applyDraftEvent, type DraftEvent } from "./draft-machine";
import type { DraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import { compareSemver, permissionKeys, proposeVersion } from "./draft-version";
import type { AiEvents, Clock, ComponentCatalog, Devkit, Differ, ProjectAccess } from "./ports";

export type PublishDeps = {
  store: DraftStore;
  devkit: Devkit;
  catalog: ComponentCatalog;
  projects: ProjectAccess;
  differ: Differ;
  events: AiEvents;
  clock: Clock;
  home: string;
};

const permissionsOf = (m: ComponentManifest): GrantedPermissions => ({
  reads: m.reads,
  writes: m.writes,
  data: m.data,
  net: m.net,
});
const firstLine = (text: string) => (text.split("\n")[0] ?? "").trim().slice(0, 200);
const same = (a: string, b: string) => Buffer.compare(readFileSync(a), readFileSync(b)) === 0;

export function createDraftPublisher(deps: PublishDeps) {
  const busy = new Set<string>();
  const paths = (d: ComponentDraft): DraftPaths => draftPaths(deps.home, d.id);
  const apply = (d: ComponentDraft, e: DraftEvent) => {
    const next = applyDraftEvent(d, e, deps.clock.now());
    deps.store.save(next);
    deps.events.publish({ type: "draft.changed", draftId: next.id, status: next.status });
    return next;
  };
  const exclusive = async <T>(id: string, work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
    if (busy.has(id)) throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "this draft is already being processed");
    busy.add(id);
    try {
      return await work();
    } finally {
      busy.delete(id);
    }
  };

  const diffOf = async (d: ComponentDraft) => {
    const p = paths(d);
    const out = [];
    for (const path of agentFiles(p, d.withServer)) {
      const before = existsSync(join(p.baseDir, path)) ? join(p.baseDir, path) : null;
      const after = existsSync(join(p.dir, path)) ? join(p.dir, path) : null;
      if (before && after && same(before, after)) continue;
      out.push(await deps.differ({ path, before, after }));
    }
    return out;
  };

  const publishInfo = async (d: ComponentDraft): Promise<DraftPublishInfo> => {
    const p = paths(d);
    const manifest = readDraftManifest(p.dir);
    const latest = d.mode === "modify" ? deps.catalog.latest(d.componentId) : null;
    const permissions = permissionsOf(manifest);
    const proposedVersion = proposeVersion(
      latest
        ? { version: latest.version, permissions: permissionsOf(latest.manifest), configVersion: latest.manifest.configVersion }
        : null,
      { permissions, configVersion: manifest.configVersion },
    );
    const granted = latest ? permissionKeys(latest.granted) : [];
    const reviewed = d.status === "permissions";
    return {
      from: latest?.version ?? null,
      proposedVersion,
      version: reviewed ? manifest.version : proposedVersion,
      changes: reviewed ? manifest.changes : d.mode === "modify" ? [firstLine(d.description)] : [],
      permissions,
      newPermissions: permissionKeys(permissions).filter((k) => !granted.includes(k)),
      usages: latest ? deps.catalog.usages(d.componentId) : [],
      hash: reviewed ? await deps.devkit.hash(p.dir) : null,
    };
  };

  const details = async (draftId: string): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails> => {
    const d = deps.store.get(draftId);
    const visible = d.status === "review" || d.status === "permissions";
    return {
      ...d,
      report: deps.store.report(draftId),
      diff: visible ? await diffOf(d) : [],
      publish: visible ? await publishInfo(d) : null,
    };
  };

  return {
    details,

    review: (input: ReviewComponentDraftInput) =>
      exclusive(input.draftId, async () => {
        const d = deps.store.get(input.draftId);
        if (d.status !== "review") throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `draft is ${d.status}`);
        const latest = d.mode === "modify" ? deps.catalog.latest(d.componentId) : null;
        if (latest && compareSemver(input.version, latest.version) <= 0)
          throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `version must be greater than ${latest.version}`);
        const dir = paths(d).dir;
        writeDraftManifest(dir, { ...readDraftManifest(dir), version: input.version, changes: input.changes });
        apply(d, { type: "reviewed" });
        return details(input.draftId);
      }),

    finalize: (input: FinalizeComponentDraftInput): Promise<PublishResult> =>
      exclusive(input.draftId, async () => {
        const d = deps.store.get(input.draftId);
        if (d.status !== "permissions") throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `draft is ${d.status}`);
        const p = paths(d);
        if (readDraftManifest(p.dir).version !== input.version)
          throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "version differs from the reviewed one");
        if ((await deps.devkit.hash(p.dir)) !== input.hash) throw new KiboError("HASH_MISMATCH", "the draft changed since review");
        if (input.target && !deps.projects.pageExists(input.target.projectId, input.target.pageId))
          throw new KiboError("NOT_FOUND", "target page not found");
        const src = deps.catalog.sourceDir(d.componentId);
        if (d.mode === "create" && existsSync(src)) {
          if ((await deps.devkit.hash(src)) !== input.hash) throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", `components/src/${d.componentId} exists`);
        }
        if (d.mode === "modify") {
          const current = await deps.devkit.hash(src);
          if (current !== (await deps.devkit.hash(p.baseDir)) && current !== input.hash)
            throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", "the component source changed since the draft started");
        }
        const install = installDraft(p.dir, src);
        let result: PublishResult;
        try {
          result = await deps.catalog.publish({ id: d.componentId, strategy: input.strategy, origin: "ai" });
        } catch (e) {
          install.rollback();
          throw e;
        }
        install.commit();
        await deps.catalog.approve({ id: d.componentId, version: input.version, hash: input.hash, trust: input.trust });
        if (input.target)
          await deps.projects.addInstance(input.target.projectId, input.target.pageId, `${d.componentId}@${input.version}`);
        apply(d, { type: "finalized" });
        removeDraft(p);
        return result;
      }),
  };
}
```
Reprise après un échec partiel : si `approve` ou `addInstance` échoue, le brouillon reste `permissions` ; une nouvelle finalisation retrouve une source identique au brouillon (accepté par les deux contrôles ci-dessus) et la phase 4 traite la republication d'une même empreinte comme un succès sans effet (C3).

```tsx
mock.module("../api", () => ({
  client: {

      return { id: "0b5c1f3e-7a51-4d2a-9c1e-2f0d6f1b8a11", status: "generating" };
```

`packages/ui/src/ai/ModifyWithAiDialog.tsx` :
```tsx
import { Button } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/button";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/dialog";
import { Label } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/textarea";
import { Bot } from "lucide-react";
import { type FormEvent, useId, useState } from "react";
import { client } from "../api";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import { AiDraftPanel } from "./AiDraftPanel";
import { useAiAvailability } from "./use-ai-availability";

type Props = {
  component: { id: string; title: string; version: string };
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
};

export function ModifyWithAiDialog({ component, open, onOpenChange }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  const { ready, block } = useAiAvailability("generateur");
  const [request, setRequest] = useState("");
  const [draftId, setDraftId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const valid = request.trim().length >= 5 && request.trim().length <= 2000;

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const draft = await client.rpc({
        method: "startComponentDraft",
        draft: { mode: "modify", id: component.id, description: request.trim() },
      });
      setDraftId(draft.id);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : fr.common.error);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className={draftId ? "sm:max-w-3xl" : "sm:max-w-lg"}>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{fr.ai.modifyTitle(component.title)}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{fr.ai.modifySubtitle(component.version)}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        {draftId ? (
          <AiDraftPanel draftId={draftId} target={null} onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={submit} className="grid gap-3">
            <Label htmlFor={`${id}-request`}>{fr.ai.modifyField}</Label>
            <Textarea id={`${id}-request`} rows={4} maxLength={2000} value={request} onChange={(e) => setRequest(e.target.value)} />
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fr.ai.modifyHelp}</p>
            {block && <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{fr.ai.blocked[block]}</p>}
            {error && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}
            <DialogFooter>
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
                {fr.common.cancel}
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" variant="agent" disabled={!ready || block !== null || !valid || busy}>
                <Bot className="size-4" /> {fr.ai.launch}
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
La modification est publiée sans cible (`target: null`) : les instances suivent la stratégie choisie à l'écran 6 (« Mettre à jour partout » ou « Créer une nouvelle version »).

`packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.test.ts` :
```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { ensureSystemProfiles, execPort, runsFromQueue, SYSTEM_PROFILES, writeKiboShim } from "./adapters";

const roots: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => {
  for (const r of roots.splice(0)) rmSync(r, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

describe("execPort", () => {
  test("runs a binary and returns its output", async () => {
    const r = await execPort([process.execPath, "--version"], 10_000);
    expect(r?.code).toBe(0);
    expect(r?.stdout).toMatch(/\d+\.\d+\.\d+/);
  });
  test("returns null for a missing binary", async () => {
    expect(await execPort(["kibo-definitely-missing-binary"], 1_000)).toBeNull();
  });
});

test("writeKiboShim forwards its arguments", async () => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "kibo-shim-"));
  roots.push(dir);
  writeKiboShim(dir, ["echo", "kibo's"]);
  expect(statSync(join(dir, "kibo")).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o700);
  const r = await execPort(["sh", join(dir, "kibo"), "component", "test", "."], 5_000);
  expect(r?.stdout).toBe("kibo's component test .\n");
});

test("ensureSystemProfiles keeps only the model of an existing system profile", () => {
  const saved = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>([
    ["generateur", { id: "generateur", model: "opus", permissionMode: "bypassPermissions", maxParallel: 4, enabled: false, system: true }],
  ]);
  const profiles = {
    get: (id: string) => saved.get(id) ?? null,
    upsert: (p: Record<string, unknown> & { id: string }) => void saved.set(p.id, p),
  };
  ensureSystemProfiles(profiles as never, "sonnet");
  expect(saved.get("assistant")).toMatchObject({ model: "sonnet", permissionMode: "default", maxParallel: 1, system: true, enabled: true });
  expect(saved.get("generateur")).toMatchObject({ model: "opus", permissionMode: "acceptEdits", maxParallel: 1, enabled: false });
  expect(SYSTEM_PROFILES.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(["assistant", "generateur"]);
});

test("runsFromQueue maps guard decisions, end events and late subscribers", () => {
  type Run = { id: string; state: string; sessionId: string | null; stdout: string; error: string | null };
  const listeners = new Set<(r: Run) => void>();
  let lastRequest: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  const runs = new Map<string, Run>();
  const queue = {
    enqueue: (req: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      lastRequest = req;
      const run = { id: "r1", state: "queued", sessionId: null, stdout: "", error: null };
      runs.set("r1", run);
      return run;
    },
    cancel: () => {},
    get: (id: string) => runs.get(id) ?? null,
    onChange: (l: (r: Run) => void) => {
      listeners.add(l);
      return () => listeners.delete(l);
    },
  };
  const ports = runsFromQueue(queue as never);
  const runId = ports.enqueue({
    profileId: "generateur",
    label: "x",
    cwd: "/tmp",
    prompt: "p",
    args: ["--tools", ""],
    env: {},
    resumeSessionId: null,
    guard: ({ toolName }) => (toolName === "Write" ? { decision: "deny", reason: "reserved" } : { decision: "allow" }),
  });
  const hook = lastRequest.onPreToolUse as (i: { tool_name: string; tool_input: Record<string, unknown> }) => unknown;
  expect(hook({ tool_name: "Write", tool_input: {} })).toEqual({ permissionDecision: "deny", permissionDecisionReason: "reserved" });
  expect(lastRequest).toMatchObject({ ticketId: null, extraArgs: ["--tools", ""], captureStdout: true });
  const ends: string[] = [];
  ports.onEnd(runId, (e) => ends.push(e.state));
  const done = { id: "r1", state: "done", sessionId: "s1", stdout: "{}", error: null };
  runs.set("r1", done);
  for (const l of listeners) l(done);
  for (const l of listeners) l(done);
  ports.onEnd(runId, (e) => ends.push(`late:${e.state}`));
  expect(ends).toEqual(["done", "late:done"]);
});
```
(Les `as never` de ce test remplacent les types réels de la phase 2 ; T0 les remplace par les vrais types si la construction d'un `RunQueue` minimal est simple.)

```ts
import { expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { KiboError, type RpcRequest } from "@kibo/schema";
import { AI_METHODS, createAiModule } from "./module";

const draftId = "0b5c1f3e-7a51-4d2a-9c1e-2f0d6f1b8a11";
function setup() {
  const seen: string[] = [];
  const mark = (name: string, value: unknown = null) => {
    seen.push(name);
    return value;
  };
  const module = createAiModule({
    ai: { status: () => mark("status", { available: true }) as never, capabilities: () => null, refresh: async () => ({}) as never },
    environment: async () => mark("environment", {}) as never,
    starter: { suggest: () => mark("suggest", { runId: "r1" }) as never },
    lifecycle: {
      start: async () => mark("start") as never,
      retry: () => {
        throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "no");
      },
      revalidate: async () => mark("revalidate") as never,
      abandon: () => mark("abandon") as never,
      openFolder: async () => mark("openFolder") as never,
      list: () => mark("list", []) as never,
      recover: async () => {},
      idle: async () => {},
    },
    publisher: {
      details: async () => mark("details") as never,
      review: async () => mark("review") as never,
      finalize: async () => mark("finalize") as never,
    },
  });
  return { module, seen };
}

test("routes every AI method and leaves the others alone", async () => {
  const { module, seen } = setup();
  expect(module.isAiRequest({ method: "listProjects" })).toBe(false);
  const requests: RpcRequest[] = [
    { method: "getAiStatus" },
    { method: "getEnvironment" },
    { method: "suggestStarter", role: "other", text: "x" },
    { method: "startComponentDraft", draft: { mode: "modify", id: "burndown", description: "Ajoute un titre" } },
    { method: "revalidateComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "getComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "listComponentDrafts" },
    { method: "reviewComponentDraft", draftId, version: "0.1.1", changes: [] },
    { method: "finalizeComponentDraft", draftId, version: "0.1.1", hash: "a".repeat(64), trust: "sandboxed", strategy: "update-all", target: null },
    { method: "abandonComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "openComponentDraftFolder", draftId },
  ];
  for (const r of requests) {
    expect(module.isAiRequest(r)).toBe(true);
    await module.dispatch(r);
  }
  expect(seen).toEqual(["status", "environment", "suggest", "start", "revalidate", "details", "list", "review", "finalize", "abandon", "openFolder"]);
  expect(AI_METHODS.size).toBe(12);
});

test("a synchronous KiboError becomes a rejection", async () => {
  const { module } = setup();
  await expect(module.dispatch({ method: "retryComponentDraft", draftId })).rejects.toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implémenter les adaptateurs et le module**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.ts` (types et noms de la phase 2 alignés en T0) :
```ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { ProfileStore } from "../agents/profiles";
import type { Run, RunQueue } from "../agents/queue";
import type { AgentRuns, Clock, Exec, GuardDecision, RunEnd } from "./ports";

export const systemClock: Clock = {
  now: () => Date.now(),
  setTimeout: (fn, ms) => {
    const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  },
};

export const execPort: Exec = async (argv, timeoutMs) => {
  const [bin, ...args] = argv;
  if (!bin) return null;
  const resolved = bin.includes("/") ? (existsSync(bin) ? bin : null) : Bun.which(bin);
  if (!resolved) return null;
  const proc = Bun.spawn([resolved, ...args], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, NO_COLOR: "1" } });
  const timer = setTimeout(() => proc.kill(), timeoutMs);
  const [stdout, stderr, code] = await Promise.all([
    new Response(proc.stdout).text(),
    new Response(proc.stderr).text(),
    proc.exited,
  ]);
  clearTimeout(timer);
  return { code, stdout, stderr };
};

const TERMINAL = new Set(["done", "failed", "cancelled"]);
const toEnd = (run: Run): RunEnd => ({
  state: run.state === "done" ? "done" : run.state === "cancelled" ? "cancelled" : "failed",
  sessionId: run.sessionId,
  stdout: run.stdout,
  error: run.error,
});

export function runsFromQueue(queue: RunQueue): AgentRuns {
  return {
    enqueue(req) {
      return queue.enqueue({
        ticketId: null,
        profileId: req.profileId,
        label: req.label,
        cwd: req.cwd,
        prompt: req.prompt,
        extraArgs: req.args,
        env: req.env,
        resumeSessionId: req.resumeSessionId,
        captureStdout: true,
        onPreToolUse: (input: { tool_name: string; tool_input: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
          let d: GuardDecision;
          try {
            d = req.guard({ toolName: input.tool_name, toolInput: input.tool_input });
          } catch (e) {
            console.error("[kibo-daemon] guard failed, denying", e);
            d = { decision: "deny", reason: "guard error" };
          }
          return d.decision === "deny"
            ? { permissionDecision: "deny", permissionDecisionReason: d.reason }
            : { permissionDecision: "allow", permissionDecisionReason: "" };
        },
      }).id;
    },
    cancel: (runId) => queue.cancel(runId),
    state: (runId) => queue.get(runId)?.state ?? null,
    onState: (runId, listener) =>
      queue.onChange((run) => {
        if (run.id === runId) listener(run.state);
      }),
    onEnd(runId, listener) {
      const now = queue.get(runId);
      if (now && TERMINAL.has(now.state)) {
        listener(toEnd(now));
        return () => {};
      }
      let fired = false;
      const off = queue.onChange((run) => {
        if (fired || run.id !== runId || !TERMINAL.has(run.state)) return;
        fired = true;
        off();
        listener(toEnd(run));
      });
      return off;
    },
  };
}

export const SYSTEM_PROFILES = [
  { id: "assistant", name: "Assistant", permissionMode: "default", workspace: "isolated", maxParallel: 1 },
  { id: "generateur", name: "Générateur", permissionMode: "acceptEdits", workspace: "isolated", maxParallel: 1 },
] as const;

export function ensureSystemProfiles(profiles: ProfileStore, defaultModel: string): void {
  for (const p of SYSTEM_PROFILES) {
    const existing = profiles.get(p.id);
    profiles.upsert({
      ...p,
      model: existing?.model ?? defaultModel,
      enabled: existing?.enabled ?? true,
      system: true,
    });
  }
}

export function writeKiboShim(binDir: string, argv: string[]): string {
  mkdirSync(binDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const file = join(binDir, "kibo");
  const quoted = argv.map((a) => `'${a.replaceAll("'", "'\\''")}'`).join(" ");
  writeFileSync(file, `#!/bin/sh\nexec ${quoted} "$@"\n`, { mode: 0o700 });
  return binDir;
}
```

`packages/daemon/src/ai/module.ts` :
```ts
import type { Environment, RpcRequest } from "@kibo/schema";
import type { DraftLifecycle } from "./draft-lifecycle";
import type { AiAvailability } from "./ports";

type Publisher = {
  details(draftId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  review(input: { draftId: string; version: string; changes: string[] }): Promise<unknown>;
  finalize(input: Extract<RpcRequest, { method: "finalizeComponentDraft" }>): Promise<unknown>;
};

export const AI_METHODS: ReadonlySet<RpcRequest["method"]> = new Set<RpcRequest["method"]>([
  "getAiStatus",
  "getEnvironment",
  "suggestStarter",
  "startComponentDraft",
  "retryComponentDraft",
  "revalidateComponentDraft",
  "getComponentDraft",
  "listComponentDrafts",
  "reviewComponentDraft",
  "finalizeComponentDraft",
  "abandonComponentDraft",
  "openComponentDraftFolder",
]);

export function createAiModule(deps: {
  ai: AiAvailability;
  environment: () => Promise<Environment>;
  starter: { suggest(input: { role: "dev" | "designer" | "pm" | "other"; text: string }): { runId: string } };
  lifecycle: DraftLifecycle;
  publisher: Publisher;
}) {
  const route = (req: RpcRequest): unknown => {
    switch (req.method) {
      case "getAiStatus":
        return deps.ai.status();
      case "getEnvironment":
        return deps.environment();
      case "suggestStarter":
        return deps.starter.suggest({ role: req.role, text: req.text });
      case "startComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.start(req.draft);
      case "retryComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.retry(req.draftId);
      case "revalidateComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.revalidate(req.draftId);
      case "getComponentDraft":
        return deps.publisher.details(req.draftId);
      case "listComponentDrafts":
        return deps.lifecycle.list();
      case "reviewComponentDraft":
        return deps.publisher.review({ draftId: req.draftId, version: req.version, changes: req.changes });
      case "finalizeComponentDraft":
        return deps.publisher.finalize(req);
      case "abandonComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.abandon(req.draftId);
      case "openComponentDraftFolder":
        return deps.lifecycle.openFolder(req.draftId);
      default:
        throw new Error(`not an AI method: ${req.method}`);
    }
  };
  return {
    isAiRequest: (req: RpcRequest) => AI_METHODS.has(req.method),
    dispatch: async (req: RpcRequest): Promise<unknown> => route(req),
    recover: () => deps.lifecycle.recover(),
    idle: () => deps.lifecycle.idle(),
  };
}
```
